```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { loadDaily, loadOverviewInput, loadPosts, MEDIA_LIMIT, readPages, rowToPost } from '@/lib/dashboard/read';

type Call = { table: string; select: string; ops: unknown[][] };

/** Fake Supabase client: every chained call is recorded; an awaited query resolves to the next result queued for its table. */
function fakeDb(results: Record<string, { data?: unknown; error?: unknown }[]>) {
  const calls: Call[] = [];
  const db = {
    from(table: string) {
      const call: Call = { table, select: '', ops: [] };
      calls.push(call);
      const chain: Record<string, unknown> = {};
      chain.select = (s: string) => ((call.select = s), chain);
      for (const op of ['eq', 'neq', 'gte', 'in', 'order', 'limit', 'range']) chain[op] = (...a: unknown[]) => (call.ops.push([op, ...a]), chain);
      chain.then = (res: (v: unknown) => unknown, rej?: (e: unknown) => unknown) => Promise.resolve(results[table]?.shift() ?? { data: [], error: null }).then(res, rej);
      return chain;
    },
  };
  return { db: db as never, calls };
}

const mediaRow = (id: string, metrics: unknown[] = [{ reach: 120, likes: 9, total_interactions: 11, captured_at: '2026-10-14T00:00:00Z' }]) => ({ id, account_id: 'a1', media_type: 'REELS', posted_at: '2026-10-10T10:00:00+00:00', permalink: 'https://www.instagram.com/reel/x/', media_metrics: metrics });

describe('loadDaily', () => {
  it('reads one account from a day on, oldest first, in 1,000-row pages, and maps columns', async () => {
    const f = fakeDb({ account_daily: [{ data: [{ day: '2026-10-13', followers: 120, reach: 50, views: 80, accounts_engaged: 4, total_interactions: 7, follows: null, unfollows: null, link_taps: 1 }] }] });
    expect(await loadDaily(f.db, 'a1', '2026-07-17')).toEqual([{ day: '2026-10-13', followers: 120, reach: 50, views: 80, accountsEngaged: 4, interactions: 7, follows: null, unfollows: null, linkTaps: 1 }]);
    expect(f.calls[0].ops).toEqual([['eq', 'account_id', 'a1'], ['gte', 'day', '2026-07-17'], ['order', 'day', { ascending: true }], ['range', 0, 999]]);
  });
  it('pages past 1,000 rows (a year or "all" is never cut at 200 days or PostgREST\'s cap)', async () => {
    const page = (n: number, start: number) => ({ data: Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => ({ day: `d${start + i}`, reach: 1 })) });
    const f = fakeDb({ account_daily: [page(1000, 0), page(1000, 1000), page(200, 2000)] });
    const rows = await loadDaily(f.db, 'a1', '2020-01-01');
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(2200);
    expect(rows[2199].day).toBe('d2199');
    expect(f.calls.map((c) => c.ops.at(-1))).toEqual([['range', 0, 999], ['range', 1000, 1999], ['range', 2000, 2999]]);
  });
  it('throws a fixed message that never echoes the database error', async () => {
    const f = fakeDb({ account_daily: [{ error: { message: 'secret detail' } }] });
    await expect(loadDaily(f.db, 'a1', '2026-07-17')).rejects.toThrow(/^dashboard read: daily failed$/);
  });
});

describe('loadPosts', () => {
  it('newest 500 feed posts with only the latest metrics snapshot each', async () => {
    const f = fakeDb({ media: [{ data: [mediaRow('m1'), mediaRow('m2', [])] }] });
    const posts = await loadPosts(f.db, 'a1', 10_000);
    expect(posts[0]).toMatchObject({ id: 'm1', accountId: 'a1', mediaType: 'REELS', reach: 120, likes: 9, comments: null });
    expect(posts[1]).toMatchObject({ id: 'm2', reach: null });
    expect(f.calls[0].select).toContain('media_metrics(');
    expect(f.calls[0].ops).toEqual([
      ['eq', 'account_id', 'a1'],
      ['neq', 'media_type', 'STORY'],
      ['order', 'posted_at', { ascending: false }],
      ['order', 'id', { ascending: false }],
      ['order', 'captured_at', { ascending: false, referencedTable: 'media_metrics' }],
      ['range', 0, MEDIA_LIMIT - 1],
      ['limit', 1, { referencedTable: 'media_metrics' }],
    ]);
    expect(f.calls).toHaveLength(1);
  });
  it('reaches back past the newest 500 only as far as `since` needs', async () => {
    const at = (iso: string) => (n: number) => ({ data: Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => ({ ...mediaRow(`${iso}-${i}`), posted_at: iso })) });
    // the newest 500 all lie after `since`: read on, and stop at the first page that reaches before it
    const f = fakeDb({ media: [at('2026-10-01T00:00:00+00:00')(500), at('2026-09-01T00:00:00+00:00')(500), at('2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00')(500)] });
    expect(await loadPosts(f.db, 'a1', MEDIA_LIMIT, '2026-09-15T00:00:00.000Z')).toHaveLength(1000);
    expect(f.calls.map((c) => c.ops.find((o) => o[0] === 'range'))).toEqual([['range', 0, 499], ['range', 500, 999]]);
    // the newest 500 already reach before `since`: one read, as before
    const g = fakeDb({ media: [at('2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00')(500)] });
    expect(await loadPosts(g.db, 'a1', MEDIA_LIMIT, '2026-09-15T00:00:00.000Z')).toHaveLength(500);
    expect(g.calls).toHaveLength(1);
  });
  it('readPages stops at max and says so', async () => {
    const full = { data: Array.from({ length: 3 }, () => ({})), error: null };
    expect(await readPages(async () => full, 'x', { size: 3, max: 6 })).toMatchObject({ truncated: true });
    expect((await readPages(async () => full, 'x', { size: 3, max: 6 })).rows).toHaveLength(6);
    await expect(readPages(async () => ({ data: null, error: { message: 'secret' } }), 'thing')).rejects.toThrow(/^dashboard read: thing failed$/);
  });
  it('drops rows with an unknown media type and tolerates non-numeric metrics', () => {
    expect(rowToPost({ ...mediaRow('x'), media_type: 'VIDEO' })).toBeNull();
    expect(rowToPost(mediaRow('y', [{ reach: 'NaN', likes: Infinity }]))).toMatchObject({ reach: null, likes: null });
    expect(rowToPost({ ...mediaRow('z'), media_metrics: null })).toMatchObject({ reach: null });
    for (const bad of ['javascript:alert(1)', 'http://instagram.com/p/x/', 'data:text/html,x', 7]) expect(rowToPost({ ...mediaRow('l'), permalink: bad })?.permalink).toBeNull();
    expect(rowToPost({ ...mediaRow('l'), permalink: 'HTTPS://instagram.com/p/x/' })?.permalink).toBe('HTTPS://instagram.com/p/x/');
  });
});

describe('loadOverviewInput', () => {
  it('hides archived accounts and loads daily + posts for each remaining account', async () => {
    const acc = (id: string, status: string) => ({ id, ig_user_id: id, username: id, status, connected_at: '2026-10-01T00:00:00Z' });
    const f = fakeDb({
      accounts: [{ data: [acc('a1', 'active'), acc('a2', 'archived'), acc('a3', 'needs_reconnect')] }],
      sync_state: [{ data: [{ account_id: 'a1', last_success_at: null, last_error_key: null, consecutive_failures: 0, daily_done_through: null }] }],
    });
    const out = await loadOverviewInput(f.db, '2026-07-17', 'UTC');
    expect(out.accounts.map((a) => a.id)).toEqual(['a1', 'a3']);
    expect(Object.keys(out.daily)).toEqual(['a1', 'a3']);
    expect(Object.keys(out.posts)).toEqual(['a1', 'a3']);
    expect(out.sync.a1.dailyDoneThrough).toBeNull();
    expect(f.calls.filter((c) => c.table === 'account_daily')).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(f.calls.some((c) => c.table === 'account_tokens')).toBe(false);
  });
});

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { CALENDAR_LIMIT, loadAudience, loadMonthMedia, loadOnline, loadPostRows, loadStories, loadTags, STORY_LIMIT } from '@/lib/dashboard/detail-read';
import { MEDIA_LIMIT } from '@/lib/dashboard/read';

type Call = { table: string; select: string; ops: unknown[][] };

/** Fake Supabase client: every chained call is recorded; an awaited query resolves to the next result queued for its table. */
function fakeDb(results: Record<string, { data?: unknown; error?: unknown }[]>) {
  const calls: Call[] = [];
  const db = {
    from(table: string) {
      const call: Call = { table, select: '', ops: [] };
      calls.push(call);
      const chain: Record<string, unknown> = {};
      chain.select = (s: string) => ((call.select = s), chain);
      for (const op of ['eq', 'neq', 'gte', 'lt', 'in', 'order', 'limit', 'range']) chain[op] = (...a: unknown[]) => (call.ops.push([op, ...a]), chain);
      chain.then = (res: (v: unknown) => unknown, rej?: (e: unknown) => unknown) => Promise.resolve(results[table]?.shift() ?? { data: [], error: null }).then(res, rej);
      return chain;
    },
  };
  return { db: db as never, calls };
}

const noStar = (calls: Call[]) => calls.forEach((c) => expect(c.select).not.toContain('*'));

describe('loadPostRows', () => {
  it('feed posts with latest metrics, caption and tag ids; null caption and permalink survive', async () => {

      media: [

          data: [
            { id: 'm1', account_id: 'a1', media_type: 'REELS', posted_at: '2026-10-10T10:00:00Z', permalink: null, caption: null, media_metrics: [{ reach: 50, likes: 3 }], media_tags: [{ tag_id: 't1' }, { tag_id: null }] },
            { id: 'm2', account_id: 'a1', media_type: 'IMAGE', posted_at: '2026-10-09T10:00:00Z', permalink: 'https://www.instagram.com/p/x/', caption: 'Hello', media_metrics: [], media_tags: null },
            { id: 'bad', account_id: 'a1', media_type: 'VIDEO', posted_at: '2026-10-09T10:00:00Z' },
          ],
        },
      ],
    });
    const rows = await loadPostRows(f.db, 'a1');
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(rows[0]).toMatchObject({ id: 'm1', caption: null, permalink: null, reach: 50, tagIds: ['t1'] });
    expect(rows[1]).toMatchObject({ id: 'm2', caption: 'Hello', reach: null, tagIds: [] });
    expect(f.calls[0].select).toContain('media_tags(tag_id)');
    expect(f.calls[0].ops).toContainEqual(['neq', 'media_type', 'STORY']);
    expect(f.calls[0].ops).toContainEqual(['range', 0, MEDIA_LIMIT - 1]);
    expect(f.calls[0].ops).toContainEqual(['limit', 1, { referencedTable: 'media_metrics' }]);
    noStar(f.calls);

  it('throws a fixed message that never echoes the database error', async () => {
    const f = fakeDb({ media: [{ error: { message: 'secret detail' } }] });
    await expect(loadPostRows(f.db, 'a1')).rejects.toThrow(/^dashboard read: post rows failed$/);

});

describe('loadStories', () => {
  it('stories in [from, to) with every snapshot oldest first; a story without snapshots keeps an empty list', async () => {
    const f = fakeDb({
      media: [
        {
          data: [
            { id: 's1', posted_at: '2026-10-14T08:00:00Z', expires_at: '2026-10-15T08:00:00Z', permalink: null, media_metrics: [{ captured_at: '2026-10-14T08:30:00Z', views: 10, exits: 1, total_interactions: 2 }] },
            { id: 's2', posted_at: '2026-10-13T08:00:00Z', expires_at: null, permalink: null, media_metrics: [] },

        },

    expect(out[0].snapshots[0]).toEqual({ capturedAt: '2026-10-14T08:30:00Z', reach: null, views: 10, replies: null, shares: null, interactions: 2, tapsForward: null, tapsBack: null, exits: 1 });
    expect(out[1].snapshots).toEqual([]);

      ['lt', 'posted_at', '2026-10-15T16:00:00.000Z'],
      ['order', 'posted_at', { ascending: false }],

  });

  it('caps at STORY_LIMIT and says so (the tab shows it; never silent)', async () => {
    const story = (i: number) => ({ id: `s${i}`, posted_at: '2026-10-14T08:00:00Z', expires_at: null, permalink: null, media_metrics: [] });
    const f = fakeDb({ media: [{ data: Array.from({ length: STORY_LIMIT + 1 }, (_, i) => story(i)) }] });
    const r = await loadStories(f.db, 'a1', 'a', 'b');
    expect(r.stories).toHaveLength(STORY_LIMIT);
    expect(r.truncated).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('loadAudience', () => {
  it('latest snapshot per kind; missing kinds are null; non-numeric counts dropped', async () => {
    const f = fakeDb({
      audience_snapshots: [{ data: [{ day: '2026-10-14', data: { '18-24|F': 10, '25-34|M': 'x' } }] }, { data: [{ day: '2026-10-14', data: { MY: 40, XK: 2 } }] }, { data: [] }],
    });
    const out = await loadAudience(f.db, 'a1');
    expect(out.age_gender).toEqual({ day: '2026-10-14', data: { '18-24|F': 10 } });
    expect(out.country).toEqual({ day: '2026-10-14', data: { MY: 40, XK: 2 } });
    expect(out.city).toBeNull();
    expect(f.calls.map((c) => c.ops[1])).toEqual([['eq', 'kind', 'age_gender'], ['eq', 'kind', 'country'], ['eq', 'kind', 'city']]);
    for (const c of f.calls) expect(c.ops).toContainEqual(['limit', 1]);
    noStar(f.calls);
  });
});

describe('loadOnline, loadTags, loadMonthMedia', () => {
  it('online followers grouped by account; no query without accounts', async () => {
    const f = fakeDb({ online_followers: [{ data: [{ account_id: 'a1', day: '2026-10-13', hourly: [1, 'x', 3] }] }] });
    expect(await loadOnline(f.db, ['a1', 'a2'], '2026-09-14')).toEqual({ a1: [{ day: '2026-10-13', hourly: [1, null, 3] }], a2: [] });
    expect(f.calls[0].ops[0]).toEqual(['in', 'account_id', ['a1', 'a2']]);
    const empty = fakeDb({});
    expect(await loadOnline(empty.db, [], '2026-09-14')).toEqual({});
    expect(empty.calls).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('tags ordered by name with explicit columns', async () => {
    const f = fakeDb({ tags: [{ data: [{ id: 't1', name: 'Promo', color: 'chart-2' }] }] });
    expect(await loadTags(f.db)).toEqual([{ id: 't1', name: 'Promo', color: 'chart-2' }]);
    expect(f.calls[0].select).toBe('id,name,color');
  });

  it('month media: posts and stories in [from, to) for the accounts', async () => {
    const f = fakeDb({ media: [{ data: [{ id: 's', account_id: 'a1', media_type: 'STORY', posted_at: '2026-10-01T00:00:00Z', permalink: null, caption: null }] }] });
    const out = await loadMonthMedia(f.db, ['a1'], '2026-09-30T16:00:00.000Z', '2026-10-31T16:00:00.000Z');
    expect(out).toEqual({ media: [{ id: 's', accountId: 'a1', mediaType: 'STORY', postedAt: '2026-10-01T00:00:00Z', permalink: null, caption: null }], truncated: false });
    expect(f.calls[0].ops.slice(0, 3)).toEqual([['in', 'account_id', ['a1']], ['gte', 'posted_at', '2026-09-30T16:00:00.000Z'], ['lt', 'posted_at', '2026-10-31T16:00:00.000Z']]);
    noStar(f.calls);
  });

  it('pages through 1,000-row pages and flags truncation only at CALENDAR_LIMIT', async () => {
    const rows = (n: number) => Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => ({ id: `m${i}`, account_id: 'a1', media_type: 'IMAGE', posted_at: '2026-10-01T00:00:00Z', permalink: null, caption: null }));
    const two = fakeDb({ media: [{ data: rows(1000) }, { data: rows(999) }] });
    const r = await loadMonthMedia(two.db, ['a1'], 'a', 'b');
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ truncated: false });
    expect(r.media).toHaveLength(1999);
    expect(two.calls.map((c) => c.ops.at(-1))).toEqual([['range', 0, 999], ['range', 1000, 1999]]);
    const full = fakeDb({ media: Array.from({ length: CALENDAR_LIMIT / 1000 }, () => ({ data: rows(1000) })) });
    const capped = await loadMonthMedia(full.db, ['a1'], 'a', 'b');
    expect(capped.truncated).toBe(true);
    expect(capped.media).toHaveLength(CALENDAR_LIMIT);
  });

  it('keeps only https permalinks (stored values are rendered as external links)', async () => {
    const row = (id: string, permalink: unknown) => ({ id, account_id: 'a1', media_type: 'IMAGE', posted_at: '2026-10-01T00:00:00Z', permalink, caption: null });
    const f = fakeDb({ media: [{ data: [row('a', 'https://www.instagram.com/p/x/'), row('b', 'HTTPS://instagram.com/p/y/'), row('c', 'javascript:alert(1)'), row('d', 'http://instagram.com/p/z/'), row('e', 5)] }] });
    expect((await loadMonthMedia(f.db, ['a1'], 'a', 'b')).media.map((m) => m.permalink)).toEqual(['https://www.instagram.com/p/x/', 'HTTPS://instagram.com/p/y/', null, null, null]);
    const story = (id: string, permalink: string) => ({ id, posted_at: '2026-10-14T08:00:00Z', expires_at: null, permalink, media_metrics: [] });
    const s = fakeDb({ media: [{ data: [story('s1', 'javascript:alert(1)'), story('s2', 'https://www.instagram.com/stories/x/')] }] });
    expect((await loadStories(s.db, 'a1', 'x', 'y')).stories.map((r) => r.permalink)).toEqual([null, 'https://www.instagram.com/stories/x/']);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Add `daysToIso` to `lib/sync/time.ts`** (right above `dayRangeSec`):

```ts
/** [first day 00:00, the day after the last day 00:00) in tz as ISO instants: the posted_at bounds of owner-timezone days. */
export function daysToIso(from: string, to: string, tz: string): { fromIso: string; toIso: string } {
  return { fromIso: dayStart(from, tz).toISOString(), toIso: dayStart(addDays(to, 1), tz).toISOString() };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Replace `lib/dashboard/read.ts`** with:

```ts
// Dashboard reads. Always the OWNER SESSION client (createServerSupabase): RLS lets the owner select, nothing else.
// Never the admin client here. Errors carry fixed messages only (Supabase messages can echo values).
import type { SupabaseClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import { supabaseAccountsStore } from '@/lib/accounts/supabase-store';
import type { StoredAccount } from '@/lib/accounts/types';
import type { DailyRow, Post } from '@/lib/analysis/types';
import type { MediaKind } from '@/lib/instagram/parse';
import { listSyncStatus, type SyncStatus } from '@/lib/sync/store';
import { dayStart } from '@/lib/sync/time';

/** Post reads always include the newest 500 (SPEC §7c backfill size; flags and heatmaps use them), then reach back as far as the Time Scope needs. */
export const MEDIA_LIMIT = 500;
/** PostgREST answers at most 1,000 rows per request (Supabase's default max-rows): a shorter page is the last page. */
export const PAGE = 1000;
/** Safety stop for one paged read. ponytail: 10,000 posts per account in one scope is far beyond any real account; raise it if one ever gets close. */
export const MAX_ROWS = 10_000;

type Row = Record<string, unknown>;
const num = (v: unknown) => (typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v) ? v : null);
const MEDIA_KINDS: readonly string[] = ['IMAGE', 'CAROUSEL_ALBUM', 'REELS', 'STORY'];

function fail(op: string): never {
  throw new Error(`dashboard read: ${op} failed`);
}

/**
 * Reads page after page with `.range(from, to)` until a page comes back short, `enough(rows)` says so, or `max` rows
 * are in (truncated = stopped there with possibly more left). The query must be ordered, so pages never overlap.
 */
export async function readPages(
  page: (from: number, to: number) => PromiseLike<{ data: unknown; error: unknown }>,
  op: string,
  o: { size?: number; max?: number; enough?: (rows: Row[]) => boolean } = {},
): Promise<{ rows: Row[]; truncated: boolean }> {
  const size = o.size ?? PAGE;
  const max = o.max ?? MAX_ROWS;
  const rows: Row[] = [];
  for (;;) {
    const { data, error } = await page(rows.length, rows.length + size - 1);
    if (error) fail(op);
    const got = Array.isArray(data) ? (data as Row[]) : [];
    rows.push(...got);
    if (got.length < size || o.enough?.(rows)) return { rows, truncated: false };
    if (rows.length >= max) return { rows, truncated: true };
  }
}

/** A newest-first media read is complete once it holds the newest `n` rows and reaches back past `since` (an ISO instant). */
export const reaches = (n: number, since: string | null) => (rows: Row[]) => rows.length >= n && (since === null || Date.parse(String(rows[rows.length - 1].posted_at)) < Date.parse(since));

export function rowToDaily(r: Row): DailyRow {
  return {
    day: String(r.day),
    followers: num(r.followers),
    reach: num(r.reach),
    views: num(r.views),
    accountsEngaged: num(r.accounts_engaged),
    interactions: num(r.total_interactions),
    follows: num(r.follows),
    unfollows: num(r.unfollows),
    linkTaps: num(r.link_taps),
  };
}

/** Permalinks are rendered as external links: keep only https URLs (a stored `javascript:` value must never reach an href). */
export const safeLink = (v: unknown): string | null => (typeof v === 'string' && /^https:\/\//i.test(v) ? v : null);

/** A media row with its embedded latest media_metrics snapshot (array of 0 or 1). Unknown media types are dropped. */
export function rowToPost(r: Row): Post | null {
  if (!MEDIA_KINDS.includes(r.media_type as string)) return null;
  const m = (Array.isArray(r.media_metrics) ? (r.media_metrics[0] as Row | undefined) : undefined) ?? {};
  return {
    id: String(r.id),
    accountId: String(r.account_id),
    mediaType: r.media_type as MediaKind,
    postedAt: String(r.posted_at),
    permalink: safeLink(r.permalink),
    reach: num(m.reach),
    views: num(m.views),
    likes: num(m.likes),
    comments: num(m.comments),
    shares: num(m.shares),
    saves: num(m.saves),
  };
}

/** Every account_daily row from `fromDay` to today, oldest first (paged: a year or "all" is more than one page). */
export async function loadDaily(db: SupabaseClient, accountId: string, fromDay: string): Promise<DailyRow[]> {
  const { rows } = await readPages(
    (from, to) =>
      db
        .from('account_daily')
        .select('day,followers,reach,views,accounts_engaged,total_interactions,follows,unfollows,link_taps')
        .eq('account_id', accountId)
        .gte('day', fromDay)
        .order('day', { ascending: true })
        .range(from, to),
    'daily',
  );
  return rows.map(rowToDaily);
}

/**
 * Feed posts + reels (stories excluded), newest first, with ONLY their latest metrics snapshot: the newest `newest`
 * (at most MEDIA_LIMIT) and, when `since` is given, every older one back to that instant (paged in steps of `newest`).
 */
export async function loadPosts(db: SupabaseClient, accountId: string, newest = MEDIA_LIMIT, since: string | null = null): Promise<Post[]> {
  const n = Math.min(newest, MEDIA_LIMIT);
  const { rows } = await readPages(
    (from, to) =>
      db
        .from('media')
        .select('id,account_id,media_type,posted_at,permalink,media_metrics(captured_at,reach,views,likes,comments,shares,saves)')
        .eq('account_id', accountId)
        .neq('media_type', 'STORY')
        .order('posted_at', { ascending: false })
        .order('id', { ascending: false })
        .order('captured_at', { ascending: false, referencedTable: 'media_metrics' })
        .range(from, to)
        .limit(1, { referencedTable: 'media_metrics' }),
    'posts',
    { size: n, enough: reaches(n, since) },
  );
  return rows.map(rowToPost).filter((p): p is Post => p !== null);
}

export interface OverviewInput {
  accounts: StoredAccount[]; // archived accounts are hidden from every view (SPEC §7c)
  sync: Record<string, SyncStatus>;
  daily: Record<string, DailyRow[]>;
  posts: Record<string, Post[]>;
}

/** Everything the Overview needs: 1 + 1 + 2 × accounts queries (more pages for long scopes), run in parallel. Posts reach back to `fromDay` in the owner's timezone. */
export async function loadOverviewInput(db: SupabaseClient, fromDay: string, tz: string): Promise<OverviewInput> {
  const [all, sync] = await Promise.all([supabaseAccountsStore(db).list(), listSyncStatus(db)]);
  const accounts = all.filter((a) => a.status !== 'archived');
  const since = dayStart(fromDay, tz).toISOString();
  const per = await Promise.all(accounts.map((a) => Promise.all([loadDaily(db, a.id, fromDay), loadPosts(db, a.id, MEDIA_LIMIT, since)])));
  return {
    accounts,
    sync,
    daily: Object.fromEntries(accounts.map((a, i) => [a.id, per[i][0]])),
    posts: Object.fromEntries(accounts.map((a, i) => [a.id, per[i][1]])),
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Replace `lib/dashboard/detail-read.ts`** with:

```ts
// Account detail, Tags and Calendar reads. OWNER SESSION client only (RLS: owner select); explicit columns, hard
// limits, fixed error messages (Supabase messages can echo values). Never the admin client, never account_tokens.
import type { SupabaseClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import type { OnlineFollowersRow, Post, Tag } from '@/lib/analysis/types';
import { MEDIA_LIMIT, PAGE, reaches, readPages, rowToPost, safeLink } from '@/lib/dashboard/read';
import type { AudienceKind } from '@/lib/instagram/metrics';
import type { MediaKind } from '@/lib/instagram/parse';

/** Newest Stories per account per range: each carries every snapshot (~48), so a year of Stories would be megabytes. The tab says when it is capped. */
export const STORY_LIMIT = 200;
export const TAG_LIMIT = 500;
/** Calendar items (posts + Stories of every account in scope) read for one range, in 1,000-row pages; past this the page says "too many". */
export const CALENDAR_LIMIT = 5000;
const ONLINE_LIMIT = 1000;
const AUDIENCE_KINDS: AudienceKind[] = ['age_gender', 'country', 'city'];

type Row = Record<string, unknown>;
const num = (v: unknown) => (typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v) ? v : null);
const str = (v: unknown) => (typeof v === 'string' ? v : null);

function fail(op: string): never {
  throw new Error(`dashboard read: ${op} failed`);
}

export interface PostRow extends Post {
  caption: string | null;
  tagIds: string[];
}

export interface StorySnapshot {
  capturedAt: string;
  reach: number | null;
  views: number | null;
  replies: number | null;
  shares: number | null;
  interactions: number | null;
  tapsForward: number | null;
  tapsBack: number | null;
  exits: number | null;
}

export interface StoryRow {
  id: string;
  postedAt: string;
  expiresAt: string | null;
  permalink: string | null;
  snapshots: StorySnapshot[]; // oldest first; empty when Instagram never shared metrics (under 5 viewers)
}

export type AudienceSnapshots = Record<AudienceKind, { day: string; data: Record<string, number> } | null>;

export interface MonthMedia {
  id: string;
  accountId: string;
  mediaType: MediaKind;
  postedAt: string;
  permalink: string | null;
  caption: string | null;
}

/** Feed posts + reels (no stories), newest first, each with its latest metrics snapshot, caption and tag ids: the newest MEDIA_LIMIT and, with `since`, every older one back to that instant. */
export async function loadPostRows(db: SupabaseClient, accountId: string, since: string | null = null): Promise<PostRow[]> {
  const { rows } = await readPages(
    (from, to) =>
      db
        .from('media')
        .select('id,account_id,media_type,posted_at,permalink,caption,media_metrics(captured_at,reach,views,likes,comments,shares,saves),media_tags(tag_id)')
        .eq('account_id', accountId)
        .neq('media_type', 'STORY')
        .order('posted_at', { ascending: false })
        .order('id', { ascending: false })
        .order('captured_at', { ascending: false, referencedTable: 'media_metrics' })
        .range(from, to)
        .limit(1, { referencedTable: 'media_metrics' }),
    'post rows',
    { size: MEDIA_LIMIT, enough: reaches(MEDIA_LIMIT, since) },
  );
  return rows.flatMap((r) => {
    const p = rowToPost(r);
    if (!p || p.mediaType === 'STORY') return [];
    const tags = Array.isArray(r.media_tags) ? (r.media_tags as Row[]) : [];
    return [{ ...p, caption: str(r.caption), tagIds: tags.map((t) => str(t.tag_id)).filter((t): t is string => t !== null) }];
  });
}

/** Stories posted in [fromIso, toIso), newest first, with every snapshot (oldest first); at most STORY_LIMIT, `truncated` when there are more.
 *  ponytail: snapshots are embedded without a per-story cap, so 200 stories x 30-minute snapshots is the ceiling;
 *  a "final numbers" rollup (Plan 12b) lifts the cap. */
export async function loadStories(db: SupabaseClient, accountId: string, fromIso: string, toIso: string): Promise<{ stories: StoryRow[]; truncated: boolean }> {
  const { data, error } = await db
    .from('media')
    .select('id,posted_at,expires_at,permalink,media_metrics(captured_at,reach,views,replies,shares,total_interactions,taps_forward,taps_back,exits)')
    .eq('account_id', accountId)
    .eq('media_type', 'STORY')
    .gte('posted_at', fromIso)
    .lt('posted_at', toIso)
    .order('posted_at', { ascending: false })
    .order('captured_at', { ascending: true, referencedTable: 'media_metrics' })
    .limit(STORY_LIMIT + 1);
  if (error) fail('stories');
  const rows = (data ?? []) as Row[];
  return { stories: rows.slice(0, STORY_LIMIT).map(rowToStory), truncated: rows.length > STORY_LIMIT };
}

function rowToStory(r: Row): StoryRow {
  return {
    id: String(r.id),
    postedAt: String(r.posted_at),
    expiresAt: str(r.expires_at),
    permalink: safeLink(r.permalink),
    snapshots: (Array.isArray(r.media_metrics) ? (r.media_metrics as Row[]) : []).map((m) => ({
      capturedAt: String(m.captured_at),
      reach: num(m.reach),
      views: num(m.views),
      replies: num(m.replies),
      shares: num(m.shares),
      interactions: num(m.total_interactions),
      tapsForward: num(m.taps_forward),
      tapsBack: num(m.taps_back),
      exits: num(m.exits),
    })),
  };
}

const isCounts = (v: unknown): v is Record<string, unknown> => v !== null && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v);

/** The latest snapshot of each audience kind (one small query per kind, in parallel). Non-numeric values are dropped. */
export async function loadAudience(db: SupabaseClient, accountId: string): Promise<AudienceSnapshots> {
  const rows = await Promise.all(
    AUDIENCE_KINDS.map(async (kind) => {
      const { data, error } = await db.from('audience_snapshots').select('day,data').eq('account_id', accountId).eq('kind', kind).order('day', { ascending: false }).limit(1);
      if (error) fail('audience');
      const r = ((data ?? []) as Row[])[0];
      if (!r || !isCounts(r.data)) return null;
      const counts = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(r.data).flatMap(([k, v]) => (num(v) !== null ? [[k, v as number]] : [])));
      return { day: String(r.day), data: counts };
    }),
  );
  return { age_gender: rows[0], country: rows[1], city: rows[2] };
}

/** online_followers rows from `fromDay` on for the given accounts, grouped by account. */
export async function loadOnline(db: SupabaseClient, accountIds: string[], fromDay: string): Promise<Record<string, OnlineFollowersRow[]>> {
  const out: Record<string, OnlineFollowersRow[]> = Object.fromEntries(accountIds.map((id) => [id, []]));
  if (accountIds.length === 0) return out;
  const { data, error } = await db.from('online_followers').select('account_id,day,hourly').in('account_id', accountIds).gte('day', fromDay).order('day', { ascending: true }).limit(ONLINE_LIMIT);
  if (error) fail('online followers');
  for (const r of (data ?? []) as Row[]) {
    const id = String(r.account_id);
    out[id]?.push({ day: String(r.day), hourly: Array.isArray(r.hourly) ? r.hourly.map(num) : [] });
  }
  return out;
}

export async function loadTags(db: SupabaseClient): Promise<Tag[]> {
  const { data, error } = await db.from('tags').select('id,name,color').order('name', { ascending: true }).limit(TAG_LIMIT);
  if (error) fail('tags');
  return ((data ?? []) as Row[]).map((r) => ({ id: String(r.id), name: String(r.name), color: String(r.color) }));
}

/** Every media item (posts AND stories) published in [fromIso, toIso) for the given accounts, oldest first, in pages.
 *  `truncated`: the read stopped at CALENDAR_LIMIT rows, so the latest days of the range may be missing. */
export async function loadMonthMedia(db: SupabaseClient, accountIds: string[], fromIso: string, toIso: string): Promise<{ media: MonthMedia[]; truncated: boolean }> {
  if (accountIds.length === 0) return { media: [], truncated: false };
  const { rows, truncated } = await readPages(
    (from, to) =>
      db
        .from('media')
        .select('id,account_id,media_type,posted_at,permalink,caption')
        .in('account_id', accountIds)
        .gte('posted_at', fromIso)
        .lt('posted_at', toIso)
        .order('posted_at', { ascending: true })
        .order('id', { ascending: true })
        .range(from, to),
    'month media',
    { size: PAGE, max: CALENDAR_LIMIT },
  );
  const media = rows.flatMap((r) => {
    const p = rowToPost({ ...r, media_metrics: [] });
    return p ? [{ id: p.id, accountId: p.accountId, mediaType: p.mediaType, postedAt: p.postedAt, permalink: p.permalink, caption: str(r.caption) }] : [];
  });
  return { media, truncated };
}
```

### Task 6: `TimeScopeBar` + `timeScope` i18n

**Files:**
- Create: `components/shared/TimeScopeBar.tsx`
- Modify: `messages/en.json`, `messages/zh-CN.json` (new top-level `timeScope`)
- Test: `tests/time-scope-bar.test.tsx` (new); `tests/messages.test.ts` must keep passing (key and placeholder parity, zh-CN really translated)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 (`addMonths`, `autoGranularity`, `daysIn`, `wholeMonths`, `DayRange`, `Granularity`), Task 2 (`GRANULARITIES`, `PRESETS`, `scopeHref`, `ResolvedScope`, `TimeScope`), `formHref`/`startProgress` (`components/shared/NavigationProgress.tsx`), `ResponsiveDialog*`, `FormField`, `Button`, `Input`, `formatDay`/`formatMonth` (`lib/format.ts`), `usePrefs`.
- Produces:
  - `TimeScopeBar({ scope: ResolvedScope; today: string; keep?: Record<string, string> })` — `keep` = the page's own parameters (tab, filters, account) that every scope link/form carries.
  - `useScopeText(r: ResolvedScope): { label: string; vs: string; note: string | null }` — `label` for the range, `vs` for `StatCard.comparison` ("vs previous 30 days", "vs same period last year", "vs September 2025", "vs 1 Sep 2026 – 17 Sep 2026", or `''` without a comparison), `note` = the coverage sentence.
  - `useBucketFormat(gran: Granularity, partial: string[] = []): (day: string) => string` — chart x labels (`formatDay` for days/weeks, `formatMonth` for months, `' *'` on partial buckets).
- The bar: presets are `<Link>`s (`aria-current="page"` on the active one; leaving "all" goes back to "previous period"); compare and group-by are native `<select>`s in GET forms (`next/form`, submitted on change, `<noscript>` button, keyed by the scope so they reset after any navigation); the dialog holds five GET forms (date range, week = any day of it, month, quarter, custom comparison days) with native date inputs (`max` = today) and selects. Every form carries the page's `keep` parameters and the parts of the scope it does not change; `startProgress(formHref(form))` starts the top bar, and the dialog closes on submit. Semantic tokens only; density tokens for control heights; wraps at 360 px.

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `components/shared/TimeScopeBar.tsx`**

```tsx
'use client';

// The Time Scope bar (creator-toolkit spec §0b): presets are links, compare and granularity are GET-form selects, and
// the custom range / week / month / quarter pickers and custom comparison days are GET forms in a ResponsiveDialog
// (a centred dialog on desktop, a bottom sheet on phones). The URL is the only state: every choice is a navigation, so
// the top progress bar and the route skeletons work unchanged. Native date inputs and selects keep it keyboard-friendly.
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import Form from 'next/form';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { CalendarRange } from 'lucide-react';
import { useTranslations } from 'next-intl';
import { usePrefs } from '@/components/providers/PrefsProvider';
import { FormField } from '@/components/shared/FormField';
import { formHref, startProgress } from '@/components/shared/NavigationProgress';
import { ResponsiveDialog, ResponsiveDialogContent, ResponsiveDialogDescription, ResponsiveDialogHeader, ResponsiveDialogTitle, ResponsiveDialogTrigger } from '@/components/shared/ResponsiveDialog';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { formatDay, formatMonth } from '@/lib/format';
import { addMonths, autoGranularity, daysIn, wholeMonths, type DayRange, type Granularity } from '@/lib/time-scope/range';
import { GRANULARITIES, PRESETS, scopeHref, type ResolvedScope, type TimeScope } from '@/lib/time-scope/scope';

const SELECT = 'h-[var(--ctl-sm)] max-w-48 min-w-0 rounded-md border border-input bg-background px-2 text-xs text-foreground';
/** How far back the month and quarter pickers go (MAX_DAYS ≈ 5 years). */
const PICKER_MONTHS = 60;

const pick = (p: Record<string, string>, keys: string[]) => Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(p).filter(([k]) => keys.includes(k)));
const omit = (p: Record<string, string>, keys: string[]) => Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(p).filter(([k]) => !keys.includes(k)));
const COMPARE_KEYS = ['cmp', 'cfrom', 'cto'];

function Hidden({ params }: { params: Record<string, string> }) {
  return Object.entries(params).map(([k, v]) => <input key={k} type="hidden" name={k} value={v} />);
}

/** Labels for a scope: what it covers, what it is compared with (StatCard "vs …"), and the coverage note. */
export function useScopeText(r: ResolvedScope): { label: string; vs: string; note: string | null } {
  const t = useTranslations('timeScope');
  const { prefs } = usePrefs();
  const days = (x: DayRange) => {
    const months = wholeMonths(x);
    if (months === 1) return formatMonth(x.from.slice(0, 7), prefs);
    if (months === 3 && (Number(x.from.slice(5, 7)) - 1) % 3 === 0) return t('quarterOption', { q: (Number(x.from.slice(5, 7)) + 2) / 3, year: x.from.slice(0, 4) });
    return x.from === x.to ? formatDay(x.from, prefs) : t('rangeText', { from: formatDay(x.from, prefs), to: formatDay(x.to, prefs) });
  };
  const rolling = 'last' in r.window;
  const label = r.preset ? t(`presetLong.${r.preset}`) : days(r.window as DayRange);
  const vs = !r.compare
    ? ''
    : rolling && r.scope.cmp === 'prev'
      ? t('vsPrevious', { days: daysIn(r.range) })
      : rolling && r.scope.cmp === 'yoy'
        ? t('vsLastYear')
        : t('vsRange', { range: days(r.compare) });
  const date = r.earliest ? formatDay(r.earliest, prefs) : '';
  const note = r.partial ? (r.earliest ? t('partial', { date }) : t('noData')) : r.comparePartial ? t('comparePartial', { date }) : null;
  return { label, vs, note };
}

/** Chart x-axis labels for buckets: the day (a week shows its first day) or the month; ' *' marks a partial bucket. */
export function useBucketFormat(gran: Granularity, partial: string[] = []): (day: string) => string {
  const { prefs } = usePrefs();
  return (day) => `${gran === 'month' ? formatMonth(day.slice(0, 7), prefs) : formatDay(day, prefs)}${partial.includes(day) ? ' *' : ''}`;
}

function Pickers({ scope, today, keep, done }: { scope: ResolvedScope; today: string; keep: Record<string, string>; done: () => void }) {
  const t = useTranslations('timeScope');
  const { prefs } = usePrefs();
  const go = (e: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    startProgress(formHref(e.currentTarget));
    done();
  };
  const thisMonth = `${today.slice(0, 7)}-01`;
  const months = Array.from({ length: PICKER_MONTHS }, (_, i) => addMonths(thisMonth, -i).slice(0, 7));
  const quarters = months.filter((m) => (Number(m.slice(5, 7)) - 1) % 3 === 0).map((m) => `${m.slice(0, 4)}-Q${(Number(m.slice(5, 7)) + 2) / 3}`);
  const fixed = typeof scope.scope.range === 'object' ? scope.scope.range : null;
  const cmp = typeof scope.scope.cmp === 'object' ? scope.scope.cmp : null;
  const carry = { ...keep, ...pick(scope.params, [...COMPARE_KEYS, 'gran']) }; // a new range keeps compare + granularity
  const row = 'grid min-w-0 grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_auto] items-end gap-2';
  return (
    <div className="grid min-w-0 gap-[var(--gp)]">
      <Form action="" onSubmit={go} aria-label={t('range')} className="grid min-w-0 gap-2">
        <Hidden params={carry} />
        <div className="grid min-w-0 grid-cols-2 gap-2">
          <FormField id="ts-from" label={t('from')}>
            {(p) => <Input {...p} type="date" name="from" required max={today} defaultValue={fixed?.from ?? scope.range.from} />}
          </FormField>
          <FormField id="ts-to" label={t('to')}>
            {(p) => <Input {...p} type="date" name="to" required max={today} defaultValue={fixed?.to ?? scope.range.to} />}
          </FormField>
        </div>
        <Button type="submit" size="sm" className="justify-self-end">
          {t('apply')}
        </Button>
      </Form>
      <Form action="" onSubmit={go} aria-label={t('week')} className={row}>
        <Hidden params={carry} />
        <FormField id="ts-week" label={t('week')} hint={t('weekDay')}>
          {(p) => <Input {...p} type="date" name="week" required max={today} defaultValue={today} />}
        </FormField>
        <Button type="submit" size="sm">
          {t('apply')}
        </Button>
      </Form>
      <Form action="" onSubmit={go} aria-label={t('month')} className={row}>
        <Hidden params={carry} />
        <FormField id="ts-month" label={t('month')}>
          {(p) => (
            <select {...p} name="month" defaultValue={months[0]} className={`${SELECT} max-w-none`}>
              {months.map((m) => (
                <option key={m} value={m}>
                  {formatMonth(m, prefs)}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          )}
        </FormField>
        <Button type="submit" size="sm">
          {t('apply')}
        </Button>
      </Form>
      <Form action="" onSubmit={go} aria-label={t('quarter')} className={row}>
        <Hidden params={carry} />
        <FormField id="ts-quarter" label={t('quarter')}>
          {(p) => (
            <select {...p} name="quarter" defaultValue={quarters[0]} className={`${SELECT} max-w-none`}>
              {quarters.map((q) => (
                <option key={q} value={q}>
                  {t('quarterOption', { q: Number(q.slice(6)), year: q.slice(0, 4) })}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          )}
        </FormField>
        <Button type="submit" size="sm">
          {t('apply')}
        </Button>
      </Form>
      {scope.preset === 'all' ? null : (
        <Form action="" onSubmit={go} aria-label={t('compareRange')} className="grid min-w-0 gap-2 border-t pt-[var(--gp)]">
          <Hidden params={{ ...keep, ...omit(scope.params, COMPARE_KEYS) }} />
          <p className="text-sm font-medium">{t('compareRange')}</p>
          <div className="grid min-w-0 grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <FormField id="ts-cfrom" label={t('from')}>
              {(p) => <Input {...p} type="date" name="cfrom" required max={today} defaultValue={cmp?.from ?? scope.compare?.from} />}
            </FormField>
            <FormField id="ts-cto" label={t('to')}>
              {(p) => <Input {...p} type="date" name="cto" required max={today} defaultValue={cmp?.to ?? scope.compare?.to} />}
            </FormField>
          </div>
          <Button type="submit" size="sm" className="justify-self-end">
            {t('apply')}
          </Button>
        </Form>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/** `keep` = the page's own URL parameters (tab, filters, account) that every scope change must carry. */
export function TimeScopeBar({ scope, today, keep = {} }: { scope: ResolvedScope; today: string; keep?: Record<string, string> }) {
  const t = useTranslations('timeScope');
  const { prefs } = usePrefs();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const text = useScopeText(scope);
  const s = scope.scope;
  const href = (next: TimeScope) => scopeHref(next, keep);
  const go = (e: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => startProgress(formHref(e.currentTarget));
  const submitOnChange = (e: { currentTarget: HTMLSelectElement }) => e.currentTarget.form?.requestSubmit();
  return (
    <section aria-label={t('label')} className="mb-[var(--gp)] grid min-w-0 gap-1.5">
      <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <nav aria-label={t('presets')} className="inline-flex max-w-full flex-wrap rounded-md border bg-card p-0.5">
          {PRESETS.map((p) => (
            <Button key={p} asChild size="sm" variant={p === scope.preset ? 'secondary' : 'ghost'}>
              <Link
                href={href({ ...s, range: p, cmp: p === 'all' ? 'none' : scope.preset === 'all' ? 'prev' : s.cmp })}
                aria-current={p === scope.preset ? 'page' : undefined}
                title={t(`presetLong.${p}`)}
                scroll={false}
              >
                {t(`preset.${p}`)}
              </Link>
            </Button>
          ))}
        </nav>
        <ResponsiveDialog open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
          <ResponsiveDialogTrigger asChild>
            <Button type="button" size="sm" variant={scope.preset ? 'outline' : 'secondary'}>
              <CalendarRange aria-hidden="true" />
              {t('custom')}
            </Button>
          </ResponsiveDialogTrigger>
          <ResponsiveDialogContent className="max-h-[90dvh] overflow-y-auto sm:max-w-md">
            <ResponsiveDialogHeader>
              <ResponsiveDialogTitle>{t('customTitle')}</ResponsiveDialogTitle>
              <ResponsiveDialogDescription>{t('customHint', { tz: prefs.timezone })}</ResponsiveDialogDescription>
            </ResponsiveDialogHeader>
            <Pickers scope={scope} today={today} keep={keep} done={() => setOpen(false)} />
          </ResponsiveDialogContent>
        </ResponsiveDialog>
        {/* key: a remount resets the uncontrolled selects after any navigation (presets, back button) */}
        <Form key={`cmp|${scope.query}`} action="" onSubmit={go} className="flex min-w-0 items-center">
          <Hidden params={{ ...keep, ...omit(scope.params, COMPARE_KEYS) }} />
          <label className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            {t('compare')}
            <select name="cmp" defaultValue={typeof s.cmp === 'object' ? 'custom' : s.cmp} disabled={scope.preset === 'all'} onChange={submitOnChange} className={SELECT}>
              <option value="prev">{t('cmp.prev')}</option>
              <option value="yoy">{t('cmp.yoy')}</option>
              <option value="none">{t('cmp.none')}</option>
              {typeof s.cmp === 'object' ? (
                <option value="custom" disabled>
                  {t('cmp.custom')}
                </option>
              ) : null}
            </select>
          </label>
          <noscript>
            <Button type="submit" size="sm" variant="outline">
              {t('apply')}
            </Button>
          </noscript>
        </Form>
        <Form key={`gran|${scope.query}`} action="" onSubmit={go} className="flex min-w-0 items-center">
          <Hidden params={{ ...keep, ...omit(scope.params, ['gran']) }} />
          <label className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            {t('gran')}
            <select name="gran" defaultValue={s.gran} onChange={submitOnChange} className={SELECT}>
              {GRANULARITIES.map((g) => (
                <option key={g} value={g}>
                  {g === 'auto' ? t('granOption.auto', { gran: t(`granOption.${autoGranularity(daysIn(scope.range))}`) }) : t(`granOption.${g}`)}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </label>
          <noscript>
            <Button type="submit" size="sm" variant="outline">
              {t('apply')}
            </Button>
          </noscript>
        </Form>
      </div>
      <p role="status" className="min-w-0 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
        <span className="font-medium text-foreground">{text.label}</span>
        {text.vs ? ` · ${text.vs}` : null}
        {text.note ? <span className="block">{text.note}</span> : null}
      </p>
    </section>
  );
}
```

### Task 7: Exports on the scope — parameters, data, sheets, report, file names

**Files:**
- Modify: `lib/export/params.ts` (`ExportParams.time`, strict Time Scope parsing, `exportWindow` removed), `lib/export/read.ts` (reads from `readFrom`, resolves the scope with the first captured day, Stories of the range), `lib/export/request.ts`, `lib/export/workbook.ts` (scope window/comparison, "Compared from/to" columns, `WorkbookOptions.period` removed), `lib/export/report.ts` (`ReportModel.compare`, `gran`, bucketed `trend` + `trendPartial`), `lib/export/filename.ts` (`exportBasename(label, range)`), `app/api/export/xlsx/route.ts`, `app/api/export/pdf/route.ts`, `app/report/page.tsx` (doc comment), `components/report/ReportDocument.tsx` (comparison text, bucketed trend), `messages/en.json` + `messages/zh-CN.json` (`export.cols.compareFrom`, `export.cols.compareTo`)
- Test: `tests/fixtures/export-data.ts` (`scopeOf`, `sampleData(q)`), `tests/export-params.test.ts`, `tests/export-read.test.ts`, `tests/export-workbook.test.ts`, `tests/export-report.test.ts`, `tests/export-pdf.test.ts`, `tests/report-pages.test.tsx`, `tests/report-document.test.tsx`, `tests/export-xlsx-route.test.ts`, `tests/export-pdf-route.test.ts`, `tests/export-client-graph.test.ts`

The export dialog is NOT touched here: it still sends `period=7|30|90`, which the new parser accepts (Task 8 moves it to the page's scope).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2 (`parseTimeScope`, `PRESETS`, `SCOPE_KEYS`, `readFrom`, `resolveScope`, `earliestDay`, `ResolvedScope`, `TimeScope`, `DEFAULT_SCOPE`, `scopeQuery`), Task 1 (`isDay`, `DayRange`, `Granularity`), Task 3 (`bucketize`), Task 4 (`summarize(…, window, …, cmp)`, `Summary.from/compare`), Task 5 (`loadPostRows(db, id, since)`, `loadStories(db, id, fromIso, toIso)`, `daysToIso`), Task 6 (`useBucketFormat`).
- Produces:
  - `ExportParams { scope: ExportScope; time: TimeScope }` (`ReportParams` extends it). `parseExportParams(q)`: exactly one `scope`; Time Scope keys strictly allow-listed — `from` (preset or real day), `to`, `cmp` (`prev|yoy|none`), `cfrom`, `cto`, `gran` (`day|week|month`), old `period` (`7|30|90`); a repeated key, an unknown value, half a pair of days or a picker shorthand (`week`, `month`, `quarter`) → `bad_params`; no range at all = the default 30 days.
  - `ExportData.scope: ResolvedScope`; `loadExportData(db, accounts, { time: TimeScope; today; tz })`.
  - `WorkbookOptions { today; now; prefs; scopeName }` (no `period`); Summary sheet columns `From`, `To` (each account's KPI window), `Compared from`, `Compared to` (empty without a comparison); Daily/Posts sheets = the scope's days.
  - `ReportModel` gains `compare: DayRange | null`, `gran: Granularity`, `trendPartial: string[]`; `period` = days in the KPI window; `trend` rows are buckets.
  - `exportBasename(label: string, range: { from: string; to: string }): string` → `in2sight-<label>-<from>_<to>`; both routes name files after `data.scope.range`.

+/** What an export accepts per Time Scope key: the canonical form the dialog sends, plus old `period=7|30|90` links. The
+ *  picker shorthands (week/month/quarter) are for the pages only. */
+const TIME_VALUE: Partial<Record<(typeof SCOPE_KEYS)[number], (v: string) => boolean>> = {
+  from: (v) => (PRESETS as readonly string[]).includes(v) || isDay(v),
+  to: isDay,
+  cmp: (v) => v === 'prev' || v === 'yoy' || v === 'none',
+  cfrom: isDay,
+  cto: isDay,
+  gran: (v) => v === 'day' || v === 'week' || v === 'month',
+  period: (v) => v === '7' || v === '30' || v === '90',
+};
+
+/** The Time Scope of an export URL, strictly: an unknown value, a repeated key or half a pair of days is rejected. No range = the default (30 days). */
+function parseTime(q: URLSearchParams): TimeScope | null {
+  for (const k of SCOPE_KEYS) {
+    const all = q.getAll(k);
+    if (all.length > 1 || (all.length === 1 && !TIME_VALUE[k]?.(all[0]))) return null;
+  }
+  if (q.has('to') !== isDay(q.get('from')) || q.has('cfrom') !== q.has('cto')) return null;
+  return parseTimeScope(q);
+}
+
 export function parseExportParams(q: URLSearchParams): Parsed<ExportParams> {
   const scope = parseScope(single(q, 'scope'));
-  const period = Number(single(q, 'period'));
-  if (!scope || !(PERIODS as readonly number[]).includes(period)) return BAD;
-  return { ok: true, params: { scope, period: period as Period } };
+  const time = parseTime(q);
+  if (!scope || !time) return BAD;
+  return { ok: true, params: { scope, time } };
 }
 
 export function parseReportParams(q: URLSearchParams): Parsed<ReportParams> {

Notes: Stories are read after the scope is resolved (for "all" the range starts at the first captured day); everything else is read in one parallel round. `ReportDocument` writes the comparison as "vs <from> – <to>" in the owner's date format — a report is a document, so it always names the days.

### Task 8: Export dialog exports the page's scope

**Files:**
- Modify: `components/export/ExportDialog.tsx` (prop `time: ResolvedScope` replaces `period`; the 7/30/90 radio group becomes a read-only "Time range" line), `lib/export/download.ts` (`ExportRequest.time`), `app/(app)/overview/page.tsx` and `app/(app)/accounts/[id]/page.tsx` (pass the page's scope; Tasks 9 and 10 then pass the fully resolved one), `messages/en.json` + `messages/zh-CN.json` (`export.period` reworded, `export.periodHint`)
- Test: `tests/export-dialog.test.tsx`, `tests/account-page.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `useScopeText` (Task 6), `parseTimeScope`, `resolveScope`, `ResolvedScope` (Task 2), the strict export parser (Task 7).
- Produces: `ExportDialog({ scopes, defaultScope, time: ResolvedScope })`; `ExportRequest { format; scope; time: string /* ResolvedScope.query, '' = default */; sections; title; note }`; `exportUrl(r, target)` = `scope` + the canonical Time Scope parameters (+ PDF/preview fields). The dialog never offers its own range: what is exported is what is on screen.

### Task 9: Overview on the scope (model, page, view)

**Files:**
- Modify: `lib/dashboard/overview.ts` (`buildOverview` takes a `ResolvedScope`; `OverviewModel.scope`/`partial`; bucketed comparison series), `app/(app)/overview/page.tsx` (parse → `readFrom` → load → resolve with the first captured day → build), `components/overview/OverviewView.tsx` (`TimeScopeBar` replaces `PeriodSelect`; comparison texts from `useScopeText`; bucket labels; account cards carry the scope), `messages/en.json` + `messages/zh-CN.json` (`pages.overview.chartSummary` reworded)
- Test: `tests/dashboard-overview.test.ts`, `tests/analysis-coverage.test.ts`, `tests/overview-view.test.tsx`, `tests/sync-progress-wiring.test.tsx`

`parsePeriod`/`fromDayFor` stay exported for now (the account page still uses them until Task 10; Task 12 removes them).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 2–6 (`parseTimeScope`, `readFrom`, `resolveScope`, `earliestDay`, `bucketize`, `DayValues`, `summarize(…, window, …, cmp)`, `loadOverviewInput(db, fromDay, tz)`, `TimeScopeBar`, `useScopeText`, `useBucketFormat`), Task 8 (`ExportDialog time`).
- Produces:
  - `buildOverview(input, o: { scope: ResolvedScope; today; now; tz; locale; weekStart: WeekStart }): OverviewModel`.
  - `OverviewModel { scope: ResolvedScope; totals; totalsSpark; sections; scopes; days: string[] /* bucket first days */; partial: string[]; series: Record<accountId, (number | null)[]> /* reach per bucket */ }` (`period` removed).
  - `OverviewView({ model, today, exportButton?, progress? })`.
- Account card links become `/accounts/<id>?<scope query>` (nothing appended for the default scope). Flags stay as before (taking off within 7 days, reach drop 7 vs 7). Sparklines stay daily (decorative).

- [ ] **Step 2: Run them to verify they fail**

Run: `npx vitest run tests/dashboard-overview.test.ts tests/overview-view.test.tsx`
Expected: FAIL — `buildOverview` ignores `scope` (`m.scope` undefined, `days` not bucketed), the view still renders the 7/30/90 links ("7 days"), card links have no query.

Notes: the comparison chart merges each account's days on the combined window's calendar days (as before) and then buckets them with sums; `chartSummary`'s select branches are two words long on purpose (`tests/messages.test.ts` treats a one-word `{branch}` as a placeholder).

---

### Task 10: Account pages on the scope (tabs keep it, Posts/Stories follow it)

**Files:**
- Modify: `lib/dashboard/read.ts` (`loadFirstDay`), `lib/dashboard/account.ts` (`buildAccountOverview` takes the scope; bucketed trend; formats over the range), `app/(app)/accounts/[id]/page.tsx` (one resolved scope for the header and the tab; each tab reads the scope's days), `components/account/AccountView.tsx` (bar outside the tab's Suspense, hidden on Audience; tabs, back link and Calendar link carry the scope), `components/account/AccountOverview.tsx` (`PeriodSelect` removed; comparison text and bucket labels from the scope), `components/account/PostsTab.tsx` (date inputs removed — the scope owns the dates; the filter form and Reset keep the scope), `components/account/StoriesTab.tsx` (`truncated` note), `messages/en.json` + `messages/zh-CN.json`
- Test: `tests/dashboard-account.test.ts`, `tests/account-view.test.tsx`, `tests/account-page.test.tsx`, `tests/posts-tab.test.tsx`, `tests/stories-audience.test.tsx`, `tests/dashboard-read.test.ts`, `tests/navigation-start.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 2–9 (`parseTimeScope`, `resolveScope`, `readFrom`, `ResolvedScope`, `bucketize`, `summarize`, `loadPosts(db, id, newest, since)`, `loadPostRows(db, id, since)`, `loadStories(…) → { stories, truncated }`, `daysToIso`, `TimeScopeBar`, `useScopeText`, `useBucketFormat`, `ExportDialog time`).
- Produces:
  - `loadFirstDay(db, accountIds: string[]): Promise<string | null>` — one indexed row (`account_daily` ordered by day, limit 1), owner session; the account page runs it in parallel with the accounts list, so the header's bar knows the coverage (and "all" its first day) before any tab streams.
  - `buildAccountOverview(input, o: { scope: ResolvedScope; today; now; tz; weekStart }): AccountOverviewModel`; `AccountOverviewModel { scope; state; kpis; trend /* buckets: reach + views summed, followers = last value */; trendPartial: string[]; formats /* posts published in scope.range */; heat; pooledAccounts; flags; lastSyncedAt; syncErrorKey }` (`period` removed).
  - `AccountView({ account, tab, scope: ResolvedScope, today: string, keep: Record<string, string>, exportButton?, children })`; tab navigation pushes `?tab=<tab>&<scope query>`.
  - `PostsTab({ …, scope?: Record<string, string> /* ResolvedScope.params */ })`; `StoriesTab({ stories, truncated?: boolean })`.
- Page parameters kept by scope links: `tab`, and on the Posts tab the validated `format`/`tag` (from `parsePostFilters`, never raw URL text).

   return rows.map(rowToPost).filter((p): p is Post => p !== null);

+/** The first day with an account_daily row for these accounts (where the captured data starts); null when there is none. */
+export async function loadFirstDay(db: SupabaseClient, accountIds: string[]): Promise<string | null> {
+  if (accountIds.length === 0) return null;
+  const { data, error } = await db.from('account_daily').select('day').in('account_id', accountIds).order('day', { ascending: true }).limit(1);
+  if (error) fail('first day');
+  const r = ((data ?? []) as Row[])[0];
+  return r ? String(r.day) : null;
+}

   sync: Record<string, SyncStatus>;

Then replace `app/(app)/accounts/[id]/page.tsx` with:

```tsx
import { Suspense } from 'react';
import { notFound } from 'next/navigation';
import { ExportDialog } from '@/components/export/ExportDialog';
import { AccountOverview } from '@/components/account/AccountOverview';
import { AccountView } from '@/components/account/AccountView';
import { AudienceTab } from '@/components/account/AudienceTab';
import { PostsTab } from '@/components/account/PostsTab';
import { StoriesTab } from '@/components/account/StoriesTab';
import { TabSkeleton } from '@/components/account/TabSkeleton';
import { setPostTagsAction } from '@/app/(app)/tags/actions';
import type { StoredAccount } from '@/lib/accounts/types';
import { requireOwner } from '@/lib/auth/require-owner';
import { buildAccountOverview, parseTab, poolFor, type Tab } from '@/lib/dashboard/account';
import { buildAudience } from '@/lib/dashboard/audience';
import { loadAccounts, loadSyncStatus } from '@/lib/dashboard/cached';
import { buildPosts, buildStories, parsePostFilters } from '@/lib/dashboard/content';
import { loadAudience, loadOnline, loadPostRows, loadStories, loadTags } from '@/lib/dashboard/detail-read';
import { exportScopes } from '@/lib/export/params';
import { loadDaily, loadFirstDay, loadPosts, MEDIA_LIMIT } from '@/lib/dashboard/read';
import { getPrefs } from '@/lib/prefs.server';
import { createServerSupabase } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { addDays, dayStart, daysToIso, localDay } from '@/lib/sync/time';
import { isUuid } from '@/lib/tags';
import { parseTimeScope, readFrom, resolveScope, type ResolvedScope } from '@/lib/time-scope/scope';

type SearchParams = Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>;

/** Live (non-archived) accounts: archived ones are hidden (SPEC §7c). Shared by the header and the tab (one query). */
const liveAccounts = async () => (await loadAccounts()).filter((a) => a.status !== 'archived');

/** One tab's data, streamed inside <Suspense>: the header and tab bar are already on screen. Each tab loads only its own data, for the scope's days. */
async function TabContent({ tab, account, sp, scope }: { tab: Tab; account: StoredAccount; sp: SearchParams; scope: ResolvedScope }) {
  const [db, prefs] = await Promise.all([createServerSupabase(), getPrefs()]); // owner session: RLS applies, never the admin client
  const now = new Date();
  const today = localDay(now, prefs.timezone);
  const tz = prefs.timezone;
  switch (tab) {
    case 'posts': {
      const [rows, tags] = await Promise.all([loadPostRows(db, account.id, dayStart(scope.range.from, tz).toISOString()), loadTags(db)]);
      const filters = { ...parsePostFilters(sp), from: scope.range.from, to: scope.range.to }; // the dates come from the scope
      // the imported server action is passed straight to the client component (never wrapped in a closure here)
      return <PostsTab posts={buildPosts(rows, tags, filters, tz)} total={rows.length} tags={tags} filters={filters} scope={scope.params} setPostTags={setPostTagsAction} />;
    }
    case 'stories': {
      const { fromIso, toIso } = daysToIso(scope.range.from, scope.range.to, tz);
      const { stories, truncated } = await loadStories(db, account.id, fromIso, toIso);
      return <StoriesTab stories={buildStories(stories, now)} truncated={truncated} />;
    }
    case 'audience':
      return <AudienceTab model={buildAudience(await loadAudience(db, account.id))} />;
    default: {
      const fromDay = readFrom(scope.scope, today);
      const pool = poolFor(account, await liveAccounts());
      const others = pool.filter((a) => a.id !== account.id);
      const [daily, posts, sync, online, otherPosts] = await Promise.all([
        loadDaily(db, account.id, fromDay),
        loadPosts(db, account.id, MEDIA_LIMIT, dayStart(fromDay, tz).toISOString()),
        loadSyncStatus(),
        loadOnline(db, pool.map((a) => a.id), addDays(today, -30)), // Instagram keeps 30 days of online_followers
        Promise.all(others.map((a) => loadPosts(db, a.id))), // best time pools the newest posts, whatever the scope
      ]);
      const model = buildAccountOverview(
        { account, sync: sync[account.id], daily, posts, online: online[account.id] ?? [], pooled: { accounts: pool.length, posts: [...posts, ...otherPosts.flat()], online: Object.values(online).flat() } },
        { scope, today, now, tz, weekStart: prefs.weekStart },
      );
      return <AccountOverview model={model} />;
    }
  }
}

export default async function AccountPage({ params, searchParams }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }>; searchParams: Promise<SearchParams> }) {
  await requireOwner(); // layouts don't re-run on client navigation, so the page checks too
  const { id } = await params;
  if (!isUuid(id)) notFound();
  const sp = await searchParams;
  const tab = parseTab(sp.tab);
  // the first captured day is one indexed row; it runs alongside the accounts list (RLS: only the owner's rows exist)
  const [accounts, prefs, first] = await Promise.all([liveAccounts(), getPrefs(), createServerSupabase().then((db) => loadFirstDay(db, [id.toLowerCase()]))]);
  const account = accounts.find((a) => a.id === id.toLowerCase());
  if (!account) notFound();
  const today = localDay(new Date(), prefs.timezone);
  const scope = resolveScope(parseTimeScope(sp, prefs.weekStart), today, first);
  const filters = parsePostFilters(sp);
  // the page's own parameters every scope link keeps (validated values only, never echoed raw)
  const keep: Record<string, string> = { tab, ...(tab === 'posts' && filters.format ? { format: filters.format } : {}), ...(tab === 'posts' && filters.tag ? { tag: filters.tag } : {}) };
  return (
    <AccountView
      account={{ id: account.id, username: account.username, displayName: account.displayName, groupName: account.groupName }}
      tab={tab}
      scope={scope}
      today={today}
      keep={keep}
      exportButton={<ExportDialog scopes={exportScopes(accounts)} defaultScope={`a:${account.id}`} time={scope} />}
    >
      {/* key: a new tab, scope or filter is a new boundary, so its skeleton shows instead of the stale tab */}
      <Suspense key={JSON.stringify(sp)} fallback={<TabSkeleton tab={tab} />}>
        <TabContent tab={tab} account={account} sp={sp} scope={scope} />
      </Suspense>
    </AccountView>
  );
}
```

### Task 11: Calendar on the scope

**Files:**
- Modify: `lib/dashboard/calendar.ts` (whole file below: `calendarRange` + `buildCalendar` replace `parseMonth`/`monthRange`/`buildMonth`), `app/(app)/calendar/page.tsx` (whole file below), `components/calendar/CalendarView.tsx` (scope bar, title from the scope, arrows = `stepScope`, the account form keeps the scope), `messages/en.json` + `messages/zh-CN.json` (`pages.calendar.previous/next` removed — the bar's `timeScope.previous/next` are used; `emptyMonth`, `truncated` reworded)
- Test: `tests/dashboard-calendar.test.ts`, `tests/calendar-page.test.tsx` (whole files below), `tests/calendar.test.tsx`, `tests/navigation-start.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `weekOf`, `daysIn`, `DayRange` (Task 1); `parseTimeScope`, `resolveScope`, `stepScope`, `scopeHref`, `ResolvedScope` (Task 2); `daysToIso` (Task 5); `loadFirstDay` (Task 10); `loadMonthMedia` (paged, Task 5); `TimeScopeBar`, `useScopeText` (Task 6).
- Produces:
  - `calendarRange(range: DayRange, weekStart, tz): { days: string[]; fromIso: string; toIso: string }` — whole weeks around the range, bounds = owner-timezone midnights.
  - `buildCalendar(range: DayRange, media: MonthMedia[], o: { weekStart; tz; today }): CalendarModel`; `CalendarModel { range: DayRange; weeks: CalendarDay[][] }`; `CalendarDay.inRange` replaces `inMonth`.
  - `CalendarView({ model, scope: ResolvedScope, today, accounts, account, truncated })`.
- Behaviour: the Calendar uses the shared scope (default = last 30 days; see Decisions). A picked month/week/quarter/range is shown whole, also when it runs past today (empty future days); a rolling preset ends today. The arrows step one period of the same kind (`stepScope`: months by months); "next" is disabled when it would start after today. Old `?month=YYYY-MM` links keep working (month picker shorthand); `?account=` is kept by every scope link and form.

Replace `tests/dashboard-calendar.test.ts` with:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { buildCalendar, calendarRange } from '@/lib/dashboard/calendar';
import type { MonthMedia } from '@/lib/dashboard/detail-read';
import { formatMonth } from '@/lib/format';

const media = (id: string, postedAt: string, mediaType: MonthMedia['mediaType'] = 'IMAGE'): MonthMedia => ({ id, accountId: 'a1', mediaType, postedAt, permalink: null, caption: null });
const OCT = { from: '2026-10-01', to: '2026-10-31' };

describe('calendarRange', () => {
  it('whole weeks around the range from the user week start; Monday vs Sunday', () => {
    const mon = calendarRange(OCT, 'mon', 'UTC'); // 1 Oct 2026 is a Thursday
    expect(mon.days[0]).toBe('2026-09-28');
    expect(mon.days).toHaveLength(35);
    expect(mon.days[34]).toBe('2026-11-01');
    const sun = calendarRange(OCT, 'sun', 'UTC');
    expect(sun.days[0]).toBe('2026-09-27');
    expect(sun.days.length % 7).toBe(0);
    expect(sun.days.at(-1)).toBe('2026-10-31');
  });

  it('any range: 30 days that start mid-week, a whole year', () => {
    const r = calendarRange({ from: '2026-09-16', to: '2026-10-15' }, 'mon', 'UTC');
    expect([r.days[0], r.days.at(-1), r.days.length]).toEqual(['2026-09-14', '2026-10-18', 35]);
    expect(calendarRange({ from: '2025-10-16', to: '2026-10-15' }, 'mon', 'UTC').days).toHaveLength(53 * 7);
  });

  it('bounds are owner-timezone midnights (Kuala Lumpur is UTC+8)', () => {
    const r = calendarRange(OCT, 'mon', 'Asia/Kuala_Lumpur');
    expect(r.fromIso).toBe('2026-09-27T16:00:00.000Z');
    expect(r.toIso).toBe('2026-11-01T16:00:00.000Z');
  });

  it('DST month in New York: the start is EDT midnight, the end is EST midnight', () => {
    const r = calendarRange({ from: '2026-11-01', to: '2026-11-30' }, 'sun', 'America/New_York'); // DST ends Sun 1 Nov 2026
    expect(r.days[0]).toBe('2026-11-01');
    expect(r.fromIso).toBe('2026-11-01T04:00:00.000Z');
    expect(r.toIso).toBe('2026-12-06T05:00:00.000Z');
  });
});

describe('buildCalendar', () => {
  it('posts bin by owner-timezone day across the range edge; stories counted apart; days outside the range marked', () => {
    const m = buildCalendar(OCT, [media('a', '2026-09-30T16:30:00Z'), media('s', '2026-10-01T02:00:00Z', 'STORY'), media('b', '2026-10-31T15:59:00Z'), media('c', '2026-10-31T16:00:00Z')], { weekStart: 'mon', tz: 'Asia/Kuala_Lumpur', today: '2026-10-15' });
    const cell = (d: string) => m.weeks.flat().find((c) => c.day === d)!;
    expect(cell('2026-10-01')).toMatchObject({ posts: 1, stories: 1, inRange: true });
    expect(cell('2026-10-01').items.map((i) => i.id)).toEqual(['a', 's']);
    expect(cell('2026-09-30')).toMatchObject({ posts: 0, inRange: false });
    expect(cell('2026-10-31').posts).toBe(1);
    expect(cell('2026-11-01')).toMatchObject({ posts: 1, inRange: false });
    expect(cell('2026-10-15').isToday).toBe(true);
    expect(m.range).toEqual(OCT);
    expect(m.weeks.every((w) => w.length === 7)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('DST fall-back in New York: 23:30 local on 1 Nov (EST, 04:30Z next day) stays on 1 Nov', () => {
    const m = buildCalendar({ from: '2026-11-01', to: '2026-11-30' }, [media('early', '2026-11-01T04:30:00Z'), media('late', '2026-11-02T04:30:00Z')], { weekStart: 'sun', tz: 'America/New_York', today: '2026-11-15' });
    expect(m.weeks[0][0]).toMatchObject({ day: '2026-11-01', posts: 2 });
  });

  it('an empty range', () => {
    const m = buildCalendar({ from: '2026-12-01', to: '2026-12-31' }, [], { weekStart: 'mon', tz: 'UTC', today: '2026-10-15' });
    expect(m.weeks.flat().every((c) => c.posts === 0 && c.stories === 0 && !c.isToday)).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('formatMonth', () => {
  it('formats a month in both languages; junk becomes a dash', () => {
    expect(formatMonth('2026-10', { locale: 'en' })).toBe('October 2026');
    expect(formatMonth('2026-10', { locale: 'zh-CN' })).toBe('2026年10月'); // 2026 nian 10 yue
    expect(formatMonth('2026-13', { locale: 'en' })).toBe('—');
  });
});
```

```tsx
import type { ReactElement } from 'react';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import type { CalendarModel } from '@/lib/dashboard/calendar';
import type { ResolvedScope } from '@/lib/time-scope/scope';

const { calls, accounts } = vi.hoisted(() => ({ calls: [] as unknown[][], accounts: [] as Record<string, unknown>[] }));
vi.mock('@/lib/auth/require-owner', () => ({ requireOwner: async () => void calls.push(['requireOwner']) }));
vi.mock('@/lib/supabase/server', () => ({ createServerSupabase: async () => (calls.push(['session']), {}) }));
vi.mock('@/lib/prefs.server', async () => ({ getPrefs: async () => ({ ...(await import('@/lib/prefs')).DEFAULT_PREFS, timezone: 'Asia/Kuala_Lumpur', weekStart: 'mon' }) }));
vi.mock('@/lib/accounts/supabase-store', () => ({ supabaseAccountsStore: () => ({ list: async () => accounts }) }));
const read = vi.hoisted(() => ({ truncated: false }));
vi.mock('@/lib/dashboard/detail-read', () => ({ loadMonthMedia: async (...a: unknown[]) => (calls.push(['media', ...a.slice(1)]), { media: [], truncated: read.truncated }) }));
vi.mock('@/lib/dashboard/read', () => ({ loadFirstDay: async (...a: unknown[]) => (calls.push(['firstDay', ...a.slice(1)]), '2026-07-01') }));

import CalendarPage from '@/app/(app)/calendar/page';

const A1 = '3f2b8c1e-5a6d-4e7f-9a0b-1c2d3e4f5a6b';
const A2 = '1a2b3c4d-5e6f-4a1b-8c2d-3e4f5a6b7c8d';
const acct = (id: string, status = 'active') => ({ id, username: id.slice(0, 4), status });
const run = (sp: Record<string, string | string[]>) => CalendarPage({ searchParams: Promise.resolve(sp) }) as Promise<ReactElement<{ model: CalendarModel; scope: ResolvedScope; account: string | null; accounts: { id: string }[]; truncated: boolean }>>;

beforeEach(() => {
  calls.length = 0;
  accounts.length = 0;
  read.truncated = false;
  accounts.push(acct(A1), acct(A2), acct('9e9e9e9e-0000-4000-8000-000000000000', 'archived'));
});

describe('calendar page', () => {
  it('requireOwner first; an old ?month= link is the month picker: whole weeks in the owner timezone over every live account', async () => {
    const el = await run({ month: '2026-09' });
    expect(calls[0]).toEqual(['requireOwner']);
    expect(el.props.model.range).toEqual({ from: '2026-09-01', to: '2026-09-30' });
    expect(el.props.scope.query).toBe('from=2026-09-01&to=2026-09-30');
    expect(el.props.account).toBeNull();
    expect(el.props.accounts.map((a) => a.id)).toEqual([A1, A2]); // archived hidden
    expect(calls.find((c) => c[0] === 'firstDay')).toEqual(['firstDay', [A1, A2]]);
    expect(calls.find((c) => c[0] === 'media')).toEqual(['media', [A1, A2], '2026-08-30T16:00:00.000Z', '2026-10-04T16:00:00.000Z']);
  });

  it('a valid account filter narrows the reads; junk, unknown or archived ids fall back to all accounts', async () => {
    expect((await run({ account: A2.toUpperCase(), month: '2026-09' })).props.account).toBe(A2);
    expect(calls.find((c) => c[0] === 'media')?.[1]).toEqual([A2]);
    for (const bad of ['x', '9e9e9e9e-0000-4000-8000-000000000000', [A1, A2]]) expect((await run({ account: bad })).props.account).toBeNull();
  });

  it('keys the view by scope and account so the selected day resets when either changes', async () => {
    expect((await run({ month: '2026-09' })).key).toBe('from=2026-09-01&to=2026-09-30|');
    expect((await run({ from: '90d' })).key).toBe('from=90d|');
    expect((await run({ month: '2026-09', account: A1 })).key).toBe(`from=2026-09-01&to=2026-09-30|${A1}`);
  });

  it('passes the truncation flag from the read', async () => {
    expect((await run({ month: '2026-09' })).props.truncated).toBe(false);
    read.truncated = true;
    expect((await run({ month: '2026-09' })).props.truncated).toBe(true);
  });

  it('no or junk parameters: the shared default (last 30 days); "all" starts at the first captured day', async () => {
    for (const sp of [{}, { month: '2026-13' }] as Record<string, string>[]) expect((await run(sp)).props.scope).toMatchObject({ preset: '30d', query: '' });
    expect((await run({ from: 'all' })).props.model.range.from).toBe('2026-07-01');
  });

  it('the running month is shown whole (the days after today stay empty)', async () => {
    const thisMonth = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7); // Kuala Lumpur and UTC agree except a few hours a month; either month starts before today
    const el = await run({ month: thisMonth });
    expect(el.props.model.range.from).toBe(`${thisMonth}-01`);
    expect(el.props.model.range.to.slice(0, 7)).toBe(thisMonth);
    expect(Number(el.props.model.range.to.slice(8))).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(28);
  });
});
```
