- [ ] **Step 3: Replace `lib/dashboard/calendar.ts`** with:

```ts
// Calendar model: whole weeks around the Time Scope's days, with the published posts and Stories binned by the
// OWNER-timezone day (SPEC section 7 "Daily boundaries ... displayed in the user's timezone"). Pure: today and tz are inputs.
import { snippet } from '@/lib/dashboard/content';
import type { MonthMedia } from '@/lib/dashboard/detail-read';
import type { WeekStart } from '@/lib/prefs';
import { addDays, daysToIso, localDay } from '@/lib/sync/time';
import { daysIn, weekOf, type DayRange } from '@/lib/time-scope/range';

export interface CalendarItem {
  id: string;
  accountId: string;
  mediaType: MonthMedia['mediaType'];
  postedAt: string;
  permalink: string | null;
  caption: string | null; // snippet
}
export interface CalendarDay {
  day: string;
  inRange: boolean; // inside the scope's days (the rest of the first and last week is dimmed)
  isToday: boolean;
  posts: number; // feed posts + reels
  stories: number;
  items: CalendarItem[]; // oldest first
}
export interface CalendarModel {
  range: DayRange;
  weeks: CalendarDay[][]; // whole weeks starting on the user's week start
}

/** The grid's days (whole weeks around the range) and the [fromIso, toIso) instants that bound them in the owner's timezone. */
export function calendarRange(range: DayRange, weekStart: WeekStart, tz: string): { days: string[]; fromIso: string; toIso: string } {
  const start = weekOf(range.from, weekStart).from;
  const end = weekOf(range.to, weekStart).to;
  const days = Array.from({ length: daysIn({ from: start, to: end }) }, (_, i) => addDays(start, i));
  return { days, ...daysToIso(start, end, tz) };
}

export function buildCalendar(range: DayRange, media: MonthMedia[], o: { weekStart: WeekStart; tz: string; today: string }): CalendarModel {
  const { days } = calendarRange(range, o.weekStart, o.tz);
  const byDay = new Map<string, CalendarItem[]>();
  const sorted = [...media].sort((a, b) => (a.postedAt === b.postedAt ? (a.id < b.id ? -1 : 1) : a.postedAt < b.postedAt ? -1 : 1));
  for (const m of sorted) {
    const at = new Date(m.postedAt);
    if (Number.isNaN(at.getTime())) continue;
    const day = localDay(at, o.tz);
    byDay.set(day, [...(byDay.get(day) ?? []), { id: m.id, accountId: m.accountId, mediaType: m.mediaType, postedAt: m.postedAt, permalink: m.permalink, caption: snippet(m.caption) }]);
  }
  const cells = days.map((day) => {
    const items = byDay.get(day) ?? [];
    const stories = items.filter((i) => i.mediaType === 'STORY').length;
    return { day, inRange: day >= range.from && day <= range.to, isToday: day === o.today, posts: items.length - stories, stories, items };
  });
  return { range, weeks: Array.from({ length: cells.length / 7 }, (_, w) => cells.slice(w * 7, w * 7 + 7)) };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Replace `app/(app)/calendar/page.tsx`** with:

```tsx
import { CalendarView } from '@/components/calendar/CalendarView';
import { requireOwner } from '@/lib/auth/require-owner';
import { loadAccounts } from '@/lib/dashboard/cached';
import { buildCalendar, calendarRange } from '@/lib/dashboard/calendar';
import { loadMonthMedia } from '@/lib/dashboard/detail-read';
import { loadFirstDay } from '@/lib/dashboard/read';
import { getPrefs } from '@/lib/prefs.server';
import { createServerSupabase } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { localDay } from '@/lib/sync/time';
import { isUuid } from '@/lib/tags';
import { parseTimeScope, resolveScope } from '@/lib/time-scope/scope';

export default async function CalendarPage({ searchParams }: { searchParams: Promise<Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>> }) {
  await requireOwner(); // layouts don't re-run on client navigation, so the page checks too
  const sp = await searchParams;
  // independent reads in parallel; the media query needs the live account ids and the scope's days, so it comes after
  const [prefs, all, db] = await Promise.all([getPrefs(), loadAccounts(), createServerSupabase()]); // owner session: RLS applies, never the admin client
  const today = localDay(new Date(), prefs.timezone);
  const accounts = all.filter((a) => a.status !== 'archived'); // archived = hidden (SPEC §7c)
  const wanted = isUuid(sp.account) ? sp.account.toLowerCase() : null;
  const account = wanted && accounts.some((a) => a.id === wanted) ? wanted : null; // unknown/archived id → all accounts
  const ids = account ? [account] : accounts.map((a) => a.id);
  // the Time Scope of the URL (an old ?month=YYYY-MM link is the month picker's shorthand); the first captured day gives "all" its start
  const scope = resolveScope(parseTimeScope(sp, prefs.weekStart), today, await loadFirstDay(db, ids));
  // a picked month, week or range is shown whole (also when it runs past today); a rolling preset ends today
  const shown = 'last' in scope.window ? scope.range : scope.window;
  const { fromIso, toIso } = calendarRange(shown, prefs.weekStart, prefs.timezone);
  const { media, truncated } = await loadMonthMedia(db, ids, fromIso, toIso);
  // key: the selected-day state lives in CalendarView, so a new range or account must remount it
  return (
    <CalendarView
      key={`${scope.query}|${account ?? ''}`}
      truncated={truncated}
      model={buildCalendar(shown, media, { weekStart: prefs.weekStart, tz: prefs.timezone, today })}
      scope={scope}
      today={today}
      accounts={accounts.map((a) => ({ id: a.id, username: a.username }))}
      account={account}
    />
  );
}
```
